*Ming Hu,Jianfu Yin,Mingyu Dou,Miaomiao Zhang,Yao Wang,Cong Hu,Bingliang Hu,Quan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 FoRIS，一个无需训练的上下文分割框架，将上下文分割（ICS）重新审视为由粗到细的渐进式细化过程，通过前景纯化、前景定位和前景整合三个阶段，逐步抑制背景干扰、定位判别性目标区域并恢复完整前景结构，在语义和部件分割任务上达到 SOTA（1-shot 和 5-shot 分别平均提升 4.5 和 4.8 mIoU）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文分割方法通常直接通过参考-查询匹配一步预测最终掩码，忽略了分割任务本身可由粗到精逐步细化的特性，导致分割结果粗糙且易受背景干扰。

Method: 从经典分割视角出发，将 ICS 视为由粗到精的渐进式细化过程，提出无需训练的 FoRIS 框架，包含三个阶段：前景纯化（抑制背景干扰）、前景定位（定位判别性目标区域）和前景整合（通过语义聚合恢复完整前景结构）。

Result: 在语义分割和部件分割任务上取得 SOTA 性能，与现有方法相比，在 1-shot 和 5-shot 设置下分别平均提升 4.5 和 4.8 个 mIoU 点。

Conclusion: 渐进式细化视角为上下文分割提供了更有效的训练无关解决方案，能够显著提升任意语义概念的精确分割性能，并已开源代码。

Abstract: In-Context Segmentation (ICS) aims to precisely segment arbitrary semantic concepts, such as objects or parts, given one or a few annotated visual exemplars. In this paper, we revisit ICS from a more classical segmentation perspective, viewing it as a coarse-to-fine progressive refinement process. Rather than directly predicting the final mask through reference-query matching, we progressively refine the segmentation from coarse and ambiguous foreground responses to precise and complete foreground structures. Building upon this perspective, we propose a training-free in-context segmentation framework, termed FoRIS. Specifically, FoRIS consists of three key stages: Foreground Purification, Foreground Localization, and Foreground Consolidation, which progressively suppress background distractions, localize discriminative target regions, and recover complete foreground structures through semantic aggregation. Experimental results demonstrate that FoRIS achieves SOTA performance across semantic and part segmentation tasks, with average improvements of 4.5 and 4.8 mIoU points over existing approaches in the 1-shot and 5-shot settings, respectively. Code: https://github.com/Xi-Mu-Yu/FoRIS.

</details>


### [24] [Exploring the Potential of Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training for Multi-Source Remote Sensing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03391)
*Xiangyang Miao,Kelu Yao,Yekai Huang,Xiaogang Xu,Junxiao Xue,Minjun Shen,Chenghui Lv,Shanji Liu,Yaying Chen,Chao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了OmniRSCLIP，一个支持RGB、SAR、多光谱（MSI）和高光谱（HSI）等多源传感器输入的端到端遥感视觉-语言对比学习框架。其核心是通过谱-空基分解（SSBD）将任意通道的适配建模为基重构问题，在不破坏CLIP预训练视觉知识的前提下扩展其输入接口；同时提出谱上下文感知的掩码对比学习来抑制模态冗余特征；并构建了首个覆盖四种模态的大规模遥感图像-文本语料库OmniRS5M。实验表明该方法在保持强RGB域性能的同时，有效将CLIP扩展到异构遥感模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感对比学习方法大多建立在面向RGB的CLIP架构上，难以利用SAR、多光谱（MSI）和高光谱（HSI）等异构传感器数据。需要一种方法将这些异构传感器纳入CLIP框架，同时避免破坏预训练视觉知识。

Method: 1) 提出OmniRSCLIP端到端对比学习框架，扩展CLIP使其支持多源传感器输入；2) 引入谱-空基分解（SSBD），将任意通道适配形式化为基重构问题：预训练CLIP的patch嵌入提供可迁移的空间基，波长条件系数在受限视觉先验空间内生成传感器特定嵌入核；3) 提出谱上下文感知的掩码对比学习机制，抑制模态特定冗余特征并增强细粒度图文对齐；4) 构建覆盖RGB、SAR、MSI、HSI四种模态的首个大规模遥感图像-文本语料库OmniRS5M。

Result: 在检索、零样本分类和语义定位等实验上，OmniRSCLIP在不将异构传感器强行映射到固定通道输入空间的情况下，将其对齐到统一的图文语义空间，既保持了强RGB域性能，又有效将CLIP推广到异构遥感模态。

Conclusion: OmniRSCLIP通过基重构的思路在保留CLIP预训练视觉知识的前提下实现了对多源遥感传感器数据的统一建模，并借助新的对比学习策略和大规模多模态语料库，为遥感视觉-语言理解提供了跨模态扩展的有效方案。

Abstract: Contrastive language-image learning (CLIP) has become a key paradigm for remote sensing vision-language understanding. However, existing remote sensing contrastive learning methods are mostly built on RGB-oriented CLIP architectures, making it difficult to exploit heterogeneous sensors such as SAR, multi-spectral imaging (MSI), and hyperspectral imaging (HSI). To address this limitation, we propose OmniRSCLIP, an end-to-end contrastive learning framework that supports multi-source sensor inputs for remote sensing vision-language modeling. The key idea is to extend CLIP beyond its fixed RGB input interface without breaking the pretrained visual knowledge. To this end, OmniRSCLIP introduces Spectral-Spatial Basis Decomposition (SSBD), which formulates arbitrary-channel adaptation as a basis recomposition problem: pretrained CLIP patch embeddings provide transferable spatial bases, while wavelength-conditioned coefficients span sensor-specific embedding kernels within a constrained visual prior space. This design avoids forcing heterogeneous sensors into a fixed-channel input space, while aligning them in a unified image-text semantic space. We further introduce a spectral-context-aware mask-based contrastive learning scheme to suppress modality-specific redundant features and enhance fine-grained image-text alignment. Finally, to support multi-modal training, we construct OmniRS5M, the first large-scale remote sensing image-text corpus covering RGB, SAR, MSI, and HSI. Experiments on retrieval, zero-shot classification, and semantic localization show that OmniRSCLIP preserves strong RGB-domain performance while effectively extending CLIP to heterogeneous remote sensing modalities.

</details>


### [25] [Neural-Collapse-guided Task-Free Continual Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03406)
*Xiaotong Kong,Chaoyang Song,Ziai Zhou,Jinxia Zhang,Kanjian Zhang,Haikun Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 NC-TFAD，一个受神经坍缩（Neural Collapse）启发的几何驱动框架，用于无任务边界的持续工业异常检测；通过将流式特征对齐到单纯形等角紧框架（ETF）原型空间、使用合成异常样本作为辅助锚点，并结合焦神经坍缩对比损失与基于正常块原型的定位分支，在 MVTec AD 和 VisA 上均优于现有的任务无关持续学习与统一异常检测基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实制造环境中的数据分布会发生不可预测的漂移，使依赖任务边界（task boundaries）的持续学习假设在实践中不成立；因此需要将工业异常检测建模为任务无关（task-free）的持续学习问题，以应对非平稳数据流、抑制表示漂移并保持正常-异常可分性。

Method: NC-TFAD 冻结预训练骨干网络，将流式特征对齐到单纯形等角紧框架（ETF）原型空间以稳定非平稳流下的表示几何；由于真实异常缺失，训练时生成合成异常样本作为辅助锚点以满足 NC 几何构造；在此基础上引入类间与类内正则化，并提出 Focal Neural Collapse Contrastive (FNCC) 损失以抑制表示漂移、提升正常与异常的可分性；定位分支由正常块原型引导，从正常训练样本构建校准的逐块偏差图，并与弱自注意力先验融合，无需像素级标注即可生成异常热图。

Result: 在 MVTec AD 和 VisA 数据集上的大量实验表明，在任务无关持续学习协议下，NC-TFAD 在图像级检测和像素级定位两方面均一致优于从通用视觉迁移的任务无关持续学习方法以及统一的异常检测基线。

Conclusion: 几何驱动建模为真实工业应用中的任务无关持续异常检测提供了一种有效且稳健的解决方案，凸显了在非平稳数据流下保持表示几何结构对持续异常检测的重要性。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed growing interest in continual anomaly detection for industrial visual inspection. However, real-world manufacturing environments exhibit unpredictable shifts in data distributions, rendering task-dependent continual learning assumptions impractical. To address this limitation, we formulate industrial anomaly detection as a task-free continual learning problem and propose NC-TFAD, a neural-collapse-inspired, geometry-driven framework for learning from non-stationary data streams without task boundaries. NC-TFAD freezes a pretrained backbone and aligns streaming features to a simplex Equiangular Tight Frame (ETF) prototype space to stabilize representation geometry under non-stationary streams. To satisfy the NC-inspired geometric construction in the absence of real anomalies, we generate synthetic anomaly samples as auxiliary anchors during training. Building on this geometry, we further introduce inter- and intra-class regularization together with a Focal Neural Collapse Contrastive (FNCC) loss to suppress representation drift and improve normal-anomaly separability. Finally, a normal-patch-prototype-guided localization branch constructs calibrated patch-wise deviation maps from normal training samples and fuses them with a weak self-attention prior, producing anomaly heatmaps without pixel-level annotations. Extensive experiments on MVTec AD and VisA show that NC-TFAD consistently outperforms representative task-free continual learning methods adapted from general vision, as well as unified anomaly detection baselines, in both image-level detection and pixel-level localization under the task-free continual learning protocol. These results highlight that geometry-driven modeling offers an effective and robust solution for task-free continual anomaly detection in real-world industrial applications.

</details>


### [26] [Mudragen: Geometrically Supervised Generation of Interacting Two-Hand Mudras for Preserving Indian Classical Dance Heritage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03415)
*Jagadish Kashinath Kamble,Jayanta Mukhopadhyay,Debaditya Roy,Partha Pratim Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MudraGen 是一个基于条件扩散模型的框架，用于从低资源数据集中合成 Bharatanatyam 中 Samyukta Hasta Mudras（双手交互手势）的逼真 RGB 图像，通过引入三类几何感知损失来保证双手姿态的解剖学合理性与协调性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度古典舞蹈手势的自动生成对舞蹈传承与保存至关重要。但该类数据集天然低资源，且许多 mudra 的梵文定义缺乏精确文本描述，使传统文本条件图像生成模型效果受限。此外，现有工作多针对简单手语或单手手势，无法处理双手交互的精细协调。

Method: 提出 MudraGen 条件扩散框架，专门面向 Bharatanatyam 的 Samyukta Hasta Mudras（双手交互手势）。核心创新是引入几何感知监督，包含三个目标：Keypoint Loss 用于 3D 关节对齐、Joint Offset Loss 用于手间空间连贯性、Shape Consistency 作为解剖正则化项，在允许双手独立姿态的同时保持手部形态一致性，从而引导扩散模型生成解剖学合理且协调的双手姿态。

Result: 实验表明，MudraGen 在视觉真实感、解剖正确性和手部姿态结构精细度上超越了现有最先进的生成方法，能够忠实复现复杂的 Samyukta Hasta mudras。除了定量指标的提升，还能生成具有文化根基、结构一致的手势。

Conclusion: MudraGen 证明了几何感知监督能显著提升双手交互手势生成的准确性与真实性，其生成的文化相关、结构一致的手势在文化保存和舞蹈教育方面具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Automatic generation of hand gestures is essential for the transmission of Indian classical dance and critical for its preservation. Indian classical dance gesture datasets are inherently low-resource, and the canonical Sanskrit definitions of many mudras lack precise textual descriptions, limiting the effectiveness of conventional text-conditioned image generation models. We present \textbf{MudraGen}, a conditional diffusion framework that synthesizes realistic RGB images of \textit{Samyukta Hasta Mudras} -- interactive two-hand gestures from Bharatanatyam (an Indian classical dance form). Unlike prior work on simple hand signs or single-hand gestures, MudraGen introduces geometry-aware supervision to capture the precise coordination, anatomical validity, and cultural nuance of interacting hands. We formulate three geometry-aware objectives: Keypoint Loss for 3D joint alignment, Joint Offset Loss for inter-hand spatial coherence, and Shape Consistency, which serves as an anatomical regularizer by encouraging consistent hand morphology while allowing independent hand poses. Together, these objectives guide the diffusion model toward anatomically plausible and well-coordinated hand configurations, enabling the synthesis of photorealistic and pose-accurate gesture images. Experimental results show that MudraGen surpasses existing state-of-the-art generative approaches in visual realism, anatomical correctness, and preservation of fine hand-pose structure, enabling faithful reproduction of complex Samyukta Hasta mudras. Beyond quantitative gains, its ability to generate culturally grounded and structurally consistent gestures highlights practical applications in cultural preservation and dance education.

</details>


### [27] [When Do Frozen VLMs Respond to Image-Free Object-Token Edits? An Answer-Key-Free Protocol and What It Reveals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03429)
*Wonbin Son,Gyumun Choi,Junil Seo,Seungmin Rho,Mi Young Lee,Hyungjoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在表征层面（对象级token）编辑场景以回答“what-if”问题的新范式，原始图像不进入VLM；同时引入一种无答案键的评测协议，系统揭示冻结VLM何时会真正响应token编辑，并在遥感数据集上验证了响应、清洁度与可读性三种结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法回答场景中的what-if问题通常以文本注入假设或使用生成模型重绘场景，这两种方式都发生在模型输入层之外。作者提出将编辑移至表征层面（对象级token），但这带来一个开放问题：冻结VLM何时会对这类token编辑产生响应？且缺乏无需后编辑答案标注的评测手段。

Method: 将图像抽象为一组对象级token，原始图像不进入VLM；引入answer-key-free协议，即不标注编辑后的答案，仅对逻辑上可确定的编辑进行评分，并通过反转每个可评分选择实现自审计。该协议揭示了三种结构：1）响应并非无偿——需要显式编辑教学（而非普通VQA训练）才能在密集场景中产生响应并放大稀疏场景中的响应；2）一旦响应开启，其受token清洁度与密度支配，可部署的检测器+分割器token在VRSBench上甚至优于oracle；3）读取是可分离的轴——无图像token路径保留了匹配patch-token基线92-96%的自由文本VQA成绩，且答案确实依赖token。

Result: 在iSAID与VRSBench两个遥感数据集及三个冻结LM骨干上，响应、清洁度与可读性三种结构均保持符号一致；可部署token与oracle相当并在VRSBench上超越oracle；无图像token路线保留92-96%的匹配VQA性能。作者公开了探针生成器、记录、评审日志与代码。

Conclusion: 冻结VLM对token编辑的响应是条件性的：需显式编辑教学开启，受token清洁度与密度调控，且读取能力可作为独立维度与文本VQA兼容。该工作为表征级场景编辑提供了可审计、可复现的评测范式与新基线，并推动无需生成式重绘的what-if问答研究。

Abstract: Answering what-if queries about a scene with a VLM usually means injecting the assumption as text or repainting the scene with a generative model. We instead move the edit to the representation level, before the model input. The image is abstracted into a set of object-level tokens, and the original image never enters the VLM. This design rests on an open question: when do frozen VLMs actually respond to such token edits? We introduce an answer-key-free protocol: no post-edit answer is annotated. It scores edits whose answers are logically determined, and audits itself by reversing each scoreable choice. The protocol reveals three structures. The response is not free: explicit edit teaching, not ordinary VQA training, produces it in dense scenes and multiplies it in sparse ones, on all three operations. Once on, it is governed by token cleanliness and density, with deployable detector+segmenter tokens competitive with the oracle and outperforming it on VRSBench. And reading is a separable axis: the image-free token route preserves 92-96% of a matched patch-token baseline's free-text VQA, and the answers measurably depend on the tokens. The response, cleanliness, and reading structures are sign-preserved across two remote-sensing datasets (iSAID, VRSBench) and three frozen LM backbones. We release the probe generator, records, judge logs, and code.

</details>


### [28] [OCR-EDR: Rendering-Aware Diagnosis and Repair for Closed-Loop OCR Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03445)
*Linnan Zhao,Kang Liu,Hao Yu,Jiabo Zhan,Chong Sun,Chen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OCR-EDR，一个"渲染感知"的OCR错误诊断与修复框架，通过联合评估预测文本及其渲染图与源图像的一致性，实现从细粒度错误诊断到迭代修复的闭环，并构建了OCRErrBench基准和DocEDR模型，显著提升了OCR诊断准确率与公式识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档OCR系统在处理复杂公式、结构化文本和长尾格式时仍易出错，可能出现遗漏细粒度内容或产生幻觉输出，且需要兼容同一视觉内容的不同等价编码。然而现有的OCR评估方法大多只给出聚合指标，难以支持案例级错误分析和针对性地改进OCR性能，缺乏从诊断到修复的完整分析手段。

Method: OCR-EDR框架以源图像、可编辑的OCR预测文本及其渲染图像为输入，首先联合判断"预测文本及其渲染"是否与源图像一致：保留有效的预测（包括渲染等价的预测），同时诊断并定位真实错误；随后施加可执行的编辑操作，并可请求更新后的渲染图像以进行迭代重评估。为实现该流程，作者构建了覆盖文本与公式、精确正例与渲染等价正例以及真实错误的OCRErrBench基准，并开发了DocEDR模型来执行"诊断—修复"循环。

Result: 在OCRErrBench上，DocEDR达到94.78%的诊断准确率；修复了86.23%的错误输入使之达到视觉一致；在DOCRcaseBench上，公式Case-F1比DOCR-Inspector-7B提升30.99个百分点；在UniMER-Test上针对四个OCR系统识别出的Bad子集，公式CDM最高提升4.62个百分点。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，OCR-EDR能够将细粒度的OCR分析转化为可验证的修正并带来实际性能提升，为OCR错误评估与迭代改进提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Although document OCR systems perform increasingly well on routine documents, complex formulas, structured text, and long-tail formats remain error-prone. OCR predictions may omit fine-grained content or hallucinate unsupported outputs, while equivalent encodings of the same visible content must be accommodated. Existing OCR evaluation methods mostly report aggregate metrics, offering limited support for analyzing case-level errors and improving OCR performance. We propose OCR-EDR (OCR Error Diagnosis and Repair), a rendering-aware framework that advances from fine-grained diagnosis to iterative repair. Given a source image, an editable OCR prediction, and its rendered image, OCR-EDR first jointly assesses whether the prediction and its rendering are consistent with the source, preserving valid predictions, including rendering-equivalent ones, while diagnosing and localizing genuine errors. It then applies executable edits and may request an updated rendering for iterative reassessment. We construct OCRErrBench from diverse real OCR predictions, covering text and formulas, exact and rendering-equivalent positives, and genuine errors, and develop the DocEDR model to execute the diagnosis--repair loop. On OCRErrBench, DocEDR achieves 94.78% diagnostic accuracy. It repairs 86.23% of erroneous inputs to visual consistency, raises formula Case-F1 by 30.99 percentage points over DOCR-Inspector-7B on DOCRcaseBench, and improves formula CDM by up to 4.62 percentage points on the identified Bad subsets of four OCR systems on UniMER-Test. These results show that OCR-EDR turns fine-grained OCR analysis into verified corrections and performance gains.

</details>


### [29] [Preserving Knowledge across Space and Time for Continual Video Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03446)
*Taehoon Kim,Jongwook Choi,Heejae Jo,Byungmin Park,Jongwon Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向持续学习场景的深度伪造视频检测框架MSFD（模态特定频率蒸馏），通过在频域中显式地将视频特征分解为空间、时间和时空三种模态，并在模型顺序更新时对各模态进行独立保留，同时引入跨模态去相关损失使时空表征与单一模态线索保持正交，从而在持续出现的各类深度伪造视频上实现更强的适应能力和更有效的性能保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量视频深度伪造的持续涌现要求检测器能够不断适应新的伪造模式。然而，现有方法主要针对深度伪造图像设计，无法捕获视频特有的线索。与仅含空间伪影的伪造图像不同，伪造视频在空间和时间轴上都会留下证据，因此在连续的模型更新过程中必须分别保留每种模态的信息，而现有方法并未做到这一点。

Method: 提出模态特定频率蒸馏（MSFD）框架：在频域中显式地将视频特征分解为空间、时间及时空三种模态，从而实现对每种模态的独立保留（因为不同类型的伪造视频在不同任务中对空间和时间线索的依赖程度各异）；并引入跨模态去相关损失，促使时空表征与单一模态线索保持正交，避免模态间的冗余干扰。

Result: 大量实验表明，在多样化的持续深度伪造视频场景下，MSFD相比现有最先进方法实现了更强的适应能力，并能更有效地保持已有任务上的性能（更少灾难性遗忘）。

Conclusion: MSFD通过频域中的模态分解与独立蒸馏，成功解决了持续深度伪造视频检测中空间/时间线索难以分别保留的问题，验证了显式解耦多模态信息对持续视频伪造检测的有效性，为该领域提供了新的研究思路和强基线。

Abstract: The continuous emergence of high-quality video deepfakes requires detectors that continually adapt to new forgery patterns, yet existing approaches, which are designed for deepfake images, fail to capture video-specific cues. Unlike deepfake images that contain only spatial artifacts, deepfake videos leave distinct evidence along both spatial and temporal axes, necessitating the separate preservation of each modality during sequential model updates. To overcome this limitation, we introduce a continual deepfake video detection framework, Modality-Specific Frequency Distillation (MSFD), that explicitly decomposes video features into spatial, temporal, and spatiotemporal modalities in the frequency domain. This decomposition enables independent preservation of each modality, as different deepfake video types exhibit varying reliance on spatial and temporal cues across tasks. Furthermore, MSFD adopts a cross-modality decorrelation loss that encourages spatiotemporal representations to remain orthogonal to single-modality cues. Extensive experiments show that our framework achieves stronger adaptation and preserves performance more effectively than state-of-the-art methods across diverse continual deepfake video scenarios.

</details>


### [30] [STARS-GS: Structure-Aware Regularized Gaussian Splatting for Large-Scale Aerial Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03447)
*Bocheng Li,Wenjuan Zhang,Jie Pan. Dongxu Han,Xuesong Ma,Yiling Yao,Yaning Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STARS-GS 是一个面向大规模航拍影像三维表面重建的结构感知 3D 高斯泼溅（3DGS）框架，通过结构感知场景划分、邻域感知高斯组织与自适应表面正则化三大策略，解决了现有方法在大型复杂场景中的分割、几何约束与正则化难题，并在大规模航拍摄影测量基准上将平均 F1 分数从 0.640 提升至 0.698（相对提升约 9.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模 3D 表面重建对地理空间制图和城市建模至关重要，3DGS 虽展现潜力但仍面临三大挑战：1) 场景划分可能将连续场景元素切分到独立优化的子区域，产生拼接伪影；2) 几何约束只关注单个高斯属性，忽视其局部组织；3) 统一的正则化难以适应异构的几何结构。

Method: 提出 STARS-GS 框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 结构感知场景划分策略，在划分时更好保留连续场景结构，并通过边界细化减少跨区域几何不一致与拼接伪影；2) 邻域感知高斯组织，将几何约束从单个基元扩展到邻域组织，促使高斯更好地贴合局部表面几何；3) 自适应表面正则化，根据局部几何特征调整正则化强度，在结构化区域促进几何一致性，同时保留非结构化区域的合理变化。

Result: 在大规模航拍摄影测量基准上的大量实验表明，STARS-GS 在表面重建上持续优于所评估的高斯类方法，将平均 F1 分数从次优方法的 0.640 提升到 0.698，相对提升约 9.1%，在几何精度和表面完整性方面均取得有效改进。

Conclusion: STARS-GS 通过结构感知的设计有效解决了大规模复杂场景中 3DGS 表面重建的划分、几何组织与自适应正则化问题，显著提升了几何精度和表面完整性，验证了结构感知策略在大规模表面重建中的有效性。

Abstract: Large-scale 3D surface reconstruction from aerial imagery is fundamental to geospatial mapping and urban modeling. Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have demonstrated considerable potential for this task. However, existing methods still face three major challenges in large and complex scenes: scene partitioning may split continuous scene elements across independently optimized sub-regions; geometric constraints mainly focus on the attributes of individual Gaussians while overlooking their local organization; and uniform regularization struggles to accommodate heterogeneous geometric structures. To address these issues, we propose STARS-GS, a structure-aware 3DGS framework for large-scale surface reconstruction. First, we introduce a structure-aware scene partitioning strategy that better preserves continuous scene structures during partitioning and reduces cross-region geometric inconsistencies and stitching artifacts through boundary refinement. Second, we develop neighborhood-aware Gaussian organization that extends geometric constraints from individual primitives to their neighborhood organization, encouraging Gaussians to better conform to local surface geometry. Third, we introduce adaptive surface regularization that adjusts the regularization strength according to local geometric characteristics, promoting geometric consistency in structured regions while preserving plausible variations in unstructured regions. Extensive experiments on large-scale aerial photogrammetry benchmarks demonstrate that STARS-GS consistently outperforms the evaluated Gaussian-based methods in surface reconstruction. It increases the average F1-score from 0.640 for the second-best method to 0.698, corresponding to a relative improvement of approximately 9.1\%, demonstrating effective improvements in geometric accuracy and surface completeness.

</details>


### [31] [Preprocessing Failure and Adversarial Detection in Depthwise-Separable Edge Vision Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03453)
*Jannatul Masruk Mukta,Rifa Sanjida,Adrita Rahman Tory,Md. Saifur Rahman,Khondokar Fida Hasan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了六种预处理防御在残差架构与深度可分离架构（边缘部署主流）上的对抗鲁棒性，发现深度可分离架构恢复能力明显更差且源于架构而非参数因素；同时揭示预处理对干净预测与对抗预测的破坏不对称性，可无重训练地用作对抗检测手段，并指出图像质量指标不能作为防御有效性的可靠代理，最终给出边缘视觉部署的决策框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预处理防御是边缘视觉系统对抗攻击的标准第一道防线，无需重训练或架构改动，且被广泛推荐为模型无关的缓解措施。然而其基础性评估都是在残差或Inception类架构上进行的，而非主导边缘部署的深度可分离卷积网络，这一未经检验的假设在安全评估文献中留下空白。

Method: 跨两类架构家族（残差类与深度可分离类）系统评估六种预处理防御在不同扰动水平下的对抗扰动恢复效果；通过消融实验区分架构性解释与参数性解释；利用预处理对干净预测与对抗预测的破坏不对称性构建无需重训练和架构改动的检测方案；并检验标准图像质量指标作为防御有效性代理的可靠性。

Result: 在所有扰动水平和防御设置下，两种深度可分离架构始终表现出较差的恢复能力，而残差架构仅部分恢复；消融结果支持架构性而非参数性解释。同时发现预处理稳定地破坏干净预测而几乎不改变对抗预测的不对称性，且标准图像质量指标无法可靠反映防御有效性；局限在于仅评估了三种架构和一种攻击族。

Conclusion: 预处理防御在边缘深度可分离架构上不能作为有效的对抗恢复机制，但它所暴露的输出分歧可用于构建无需重训练的对抗检测手段；标准图像质量指标不应作为防御评估代理；作者据此为具有对抗韧性的边缘视觉部署提供了实践者决策框架。

Abstract: Preprocessing-based defenses are the standard first-line response to adversarial attacks on edge vision systems, requiring no retraining, no architectural changes, and widely recommended as model-agnostic mitigations. Yet the foundational evaluations of these defenses were conducted on residual or Inception-class architectures, not on the depthwise-separable CNNs that dominate edge deployments. This untested assumption leaves a gap in the security evaluation literature. This paper closes that gap by evaluating six preprocessing defenses against adversarial perturbations across both architecture families. Across all perturbation levels and defenses tested, the two depthwise-separable architectures show consistently poor recovery while the residual architecture shows partial recovery; ablation results are consistent with an architectural rather than parametric explanation, though only three architectures and one attack family are evaluated. Crucially, this failure is not merely a negative result. The same output divergence that disqualifies preprocessing as a recovery mechanism reveals a detection opportunity: preprocessing consistently disrupts clean predictions while leaving adversarial predictions largely unchanged, an asymmetry that is directly measurable without retraining or architectural modification. We further show that standard image quality metrics are unreliable proxies for defense effectiveness, a methodological gap in current evaluation practice. A practitioner decision framework is provided for adversarially resilient edge vision deployment.

</details>


### [32] [BMCTrack-d: Pig re-identification and tracking via back marks in challenging camera settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03463)
*David Brunner,Maciej Oczak,Marie Bordes,Jean-Loup Rault,Stephan M. Winkler,Viktoria Dorfer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 BMCTrack-d，一种基于检测的猪只追踪方法，利用独特的背部标记实现侧视、低分辨率、强遮挡和快速运动场景下的个体级猪只再识别与追踪，通过神经网络背部标记分类器及时间一致性校验和去重两个后处理阶段，在测试集上超越两个强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个体级猪只监测需要可靠的长期身份识别与追踪，但同品种猪外观高度相似，且现有研究几乎仅在俯视摄像设置下进行（便于追踪但不一定可行）。因此需要在具有挑战的侧视摄像机设置下，实现稳健的猪只再识别与追踪。

Method: 提出一种新的基于检测的追踪（tracking-by-detection）方法 BMCTrack-d：首先利用基于神经网络的背部标记分类器预测已检测猪只的身份；随后引入两个专门的后处理阶段提升长期再识别的可靠性——时间预测一致性检查（依据近期预测历史验证身份分配）和去重（解决每个时间步内冲突的身份分配）。该方法明确优先考虑基于外观的准确再识别，而非连续追踪。

Result: 在一个极具挑战性的测试集上，BMCTrack-d 在更高阶追踪精度上分别超过两个强基线 BoT-SORT-ReID 和 TrackTrack-ReID 达 9.11% 和 1.03%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于背部标记的再识别与追踪方法能有效支持挑战性场景下稳健的个体级猪只监测，弥补了现有追踪器在个体级监测场景中的关键局限。

Abstract: Automated pig monitoring is essential for assessing their health, behaviour, and welfare. To date, most pig monitoring solutions operate on the group-level, because individual-level monitoring requires reliable long-term identification and tracking of each animal. For domesticated pigs this remains challenging because pigs of the same breed often have highly uniform appearances. Moreover, research on pig monitoring is almost exclusively reported in top-down view camera settings, which considerably ease tracking, but are not always an option in practice. In this work, BMCTrack-d is presented, a novel tracking-by-detection approach that leverages unique back marks to enable robust pig re-identification and tracking in a challenging side-view camera setting, afflicted by rapidly moving pigs, severe occlusions and low resolution. The method first predicts the detected pigs' identities using a neural network-based back mark classifier. To improve re-identification reliability over time, two dedicated post-processing stages are introduced: a temporal prediction consistency check, which validates the identity assignments against the recent prediction history, and deduplication, which resolves conflicting identity assignments in each time step. By explicitly prioritising accurate, appearance-based re-identification over continuous tracking, the proposed approach addresses a key limitation of existing trackers for individual-level monitoring scenarios. On a demanding test set BMCTrack-d outperforms two strong baselines, BoT-SORT-ReID and TrackTrack-ReID, by 9.11% and 1.03%, respectively, in higher-order tracking accuracy. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of back mark-based re-identification and tracking for robust individual-level pig monitoring in challenging settings.

</details>


### [33] [SafeRestore: Detector-Relative Risk Certificates for Selective Industrial Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03475)
*Shaoliang Yang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SafeRestore，将工业检测流水线中的图像复原建模为选择性动作问题（在原始测量图像与五个复原候选之间选择），通过动作特定的拟合评分、阈值数据上的门控选择以及在不相交认证样本上的两个单侧精确二项界（证据损失率与过量激活率）提供可审计的保证。回顾性实验表明其可审计的风险-覆盖行为，但自适应路由在现有证据上并未优于更简单的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业检测流水线通常在检测器作用于图像之前先对测量图像进行复原，但复原可能抑制检测器所依赖的缺陷结构，或在干净区域诱发不必要的激活。作者因此将复原决策形式化为选择性动作问题，而非无条件地默认复原。

Method: SafeRestore 在测量显示与五个复原候选之间做选择性动作选择：用动作特定的拟合分数对候选项排序，在阈值调优数据上选择门控策略，并在不相交的认证样本上以两个单侧精确二项检验评估固定门控——一个针对正条件证据损失事件率，一个针对全部接受集合的过量激活事件率。保证是在图像级 i.i.d. 工作模型下、针对观察认证结果之前固定的单一策略的边缘性保证。

Result: 在 4,591 张公开 Carinthia-S 图像的回顾性拆分样本研究中，主全动作策略在五次训练重复中仅一次通过（失败计零时门控测试覆盖率为 12.0% ± 26.9%）；固定 bicubic 与降复杂度变体通过更频繁。在保留形态上，证据损失率升至 81.1–90.3%，且 KolektorSDD 缺乏检测器能力及足够的目标正样本认证图像。

Conclusion: 核心贡献是一个可审计的、面向检测器的框架，用于决定变换后的图像何时可自动返回、何时必须人工复核；作者明确不声称自适应路由在现有证据上优于更简单的策略。

Abstract: Industrial inspection pipelines often restore a measured image before a detector acts on it, yet restoration can suppress detector-supported defect structure or create clean-region activations. We formulate restoration as a selective action problem over the measured display, five restored candidates, and review. SafeRestore ranks candidates with action-specific fitted scores, chooses a gate on threshold-tuning data, and evaluates the fixed gate on a disjoint certification sample with two one-sided exact binomial bounds: one for the positive-conditional evidence-loss incident rate and one for the all-accepted excess-activation incident rate. The guarantee is marginal for one policy fixed before its certification outcomes are observed, under an image-level i.i.d. working model. In a retrospective split-sample study of 4,591 public Carinthia-S images, the protocol yields auditable risk-coverage behavior. The primary all-action policy passes in one of five training repetitions (12.0% +/- 26.9% pass-gated test coverage when failures count as zero), whereas fixed bicubic and reduced-complexity variants pass more often. On reserved morphologies, evidence-loss incidence rises to 81.1-90.3%, and KolektorSDD lacks both detector competence and enough positive certification images for the stated target. The contribution is therefore an auditable, detector-relative framework for deciding when a transformed image may be returned automatically and when review remains necessary -- not a claim that adaptive routing outperforms simpler policies on the present evidence.

</details>


### [34] [Tree species mapping in Denmark: A comparison of spectral-temporal features with geospatial foundation model embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03480)
*Alkiviadis Koukos,Spyros Kondylatos,Thomas Nord-Larsen,Lotte Nyborg,Christian Tøttrup,Kenneth Grogan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 利用丹麦国家森林清查样地数据与对地观测数据绘制全国树种分布图，系统比较了人工设计的谱时特征（STF）与基础模型（TESSERA、AlphaEarth）嵌入表示在树种分类中的表现，并生成了丹麦首个10米分辨率全国树种图（总体精度79.9%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模森林特征刻画需要高分辨率、可扩展的树种制图方法；传统方法依赖人工设计特征且在小样本条件下性能受限。本文旨在评估基础模型（foundation models）能否替代人工设计的谱时特征，以实现全国尺度树种分类，并填补丹麦缺乏高分辨率全国树种图的空白。

Method: 基于丹麦国家森林清查（NFI）样地数据与对地观测数据开展树种分类。对比两类输入表示：（i）由多时相Sentinel-1和Sentinel-2观测导出的手工谱时特征（STF）；（ii）由EO基础模型TESSERA和AlphaEarth生成的嵌入特征；两种表示均补充冠层高度信息。采用随机森林、XGBoost和多层感知机（MLP）三种分类器，并分别评估纯林与混交林样地；通过消融实验和训练样本量分析检验各数据源贡献，最终将最优模型应用于全国尺度生成10 m树种图。

Result: 基于STF的MLP分类性能最高，纯林与混交林的宏F1分数分别为0.843和0.653；基于TESSERA嵌入的MLP在纯林上表现接近最优（差距在1.1个百分点以内）。当训练样地少于约25%时，TESSERA一致优于STF模型，在小样本条件下优势显著。多年观测系统性优于单年输入，消融实验显示Sentinel-1后向散射、光谱指数与冠层高度具有互补贡献。最优模型在全国尺度生成10 m树种图，面积调整验证的总体精度为79.9%。

Conclusion: 本文生成的丹麦首幅高分辨率全国树种图以开放获取形式发布，为基础模型在大型森林特征刻画中的应用提供了实证：基础模型嵌入在小样本和可迁移场景下尤为有价值，而人工谱时特征仍在大样本下保持精度优势；该图可作为森林监测、生态研究与土地管理的宝贵资源。

Abstract: We map tree species across Denmark using National Forest Inventory plots and EO data, while evaluating the potential of foundation models for large-scale forest characterization. We compare two alternative input representations for tree species classification: (i) manually engineered spectral-temporal features (STF) derived from multi-temporal Sentinel-1 and Sentinel-2 observations, and (ii) embeddings generated by the EO FMs TESSERA and AlphaEarth. Both representations are complemented with canopy height information. Random forest, XGBoost, and Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) classifiers are evaluated for all input representations, with separate assessments for pure and mixed forest stands. The STF-based MLP achieves the highest classification performance, yielding macro F1 scores of 0.843 and 0.653 for pure and mixed stands, respectively. The MLP trained on TESSERA embeddings delivers competitive performance for pure stands, achieving results within 1.1 percentage points of the best-performing model. TESSERA consistently outperforms STF-based models when fewer than approximately 25% of training plots are available, demonstrating a substantial advantage under limited training data. Multi-year observations systematically improve classification accuracy relative to single-year inputs, while ablation experiments reveal the complementary contributions of Sentinel-1 backscatter, spectral indices, and canopy height data. The best-performing model is subsequently applied at the national scale to generate a 10 m tree species map of Denmark. Area-adjusted validation indicates an overall map accuracy of 79.9%. The resulting map, released as an open-access product, is the first high-resolution national tree species map of Denmark and provides a valuable resource for forest monitoring, ecological research, and land management applications.

</details>

*Yue Zhao,Hua Yu,Yukun Zhao,Yuzhi Zhang,Maoguo Gong,Xin Mei,Zhuping Hu,Yanchi Li,A. K. Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 FlexibleFusion，一个面向红外-可见光目标检测（IVOD）的统一自适应方法，能够在完整模态与缺失模态情况下无缝工作。其核心包括模态感知专家协作（MAEC）机制，可在可用时进行跨模态融合、缺失时退化为自融合；以及残差自步进熵最优传输（RSPEOT），从传输视角对齐异构特征分布并缓解标准EOT的优化负担。实验表明在任意模态配置下均有一致性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红外-可见光目标检测方法通常假设两种模态始终存在，固定的融合策略在某一模态缺失时会失效；同时，在光谱分布差异显著的情况下可靠估计跨模态的语义相关性仍是关键挑战。传感器的失效或丢帧使得上述问题在实践中普遍存在。

Method: FlexibleFusion 采用统一自适应框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 模态感知专家协作（MAEC）机制，选择性激活并聚合跨模态或模态内专家通路——双模态完整时执行跨模态融合，缺失时退化为自融合；2) 残差自步进熵最优传输（RSPEOT），从传输角度对齐异构特征分布，以残差驱动的自步进更新替代标准EOT的固定稀疏系数，优先匹配可靠样本并逐步精化困难样本，既保证可靠的语义对齐又减轻优化负担。

Result: 在完整模态与缺失模态两种协议下的全面实验表明，该方法在任意模态配置下均能获得一致且稳定的性能，验证了其对不同模态可用性的适应能力。

Conclusion: FlexibleFusion 是一种统一且自适应的检测方法，可在完整的模态可用性与部分缺失之间灵活分配集成通路与融合强度，克服了传统固定融合在单模态缺失时失效的局限；同时 RSPEOT 有效解决了跨模态语义对齐的挑战。代码将在论文发表后公开。

Abstract: Infrared-visible object detection (IVOD) integrates complementary evidence from visible and infrared sensors for reliable perception in challenging scenes. In practice, sensors may fail or drop frames, leaving one modality unavailable or intermittent. Existing methods for IVOD assume both modalities are always present, and fixed fusion collapses when one stream is missing. Furthermore, it remains a critical challenge to reliably estimate semantic correlation across heterogeneous modalities, especially under spectral distribution discrepancy. We present FlexibleFusion, a unified and adaptive method that flexibly allocates integration pathways and fusion strength, operating seamlessly across complete and missing-modality regimes. At its core, the Modality-Aware Experts Collaboration (MAEC) mechanism selectively activates and aggregates cross-modal or intra-modal expert pathways. It allows cross-modal fusion when full modalities are available and falls back to self-fusion under missing conditions. Additionally, we design Residual Self-Paced Entropic Optimal Transport (RSPEOT) to align heterogeneous feature distributions from a transport perspective. Instead of relying on the fixed sparsity coefficient in standard entropic optimal transport (EOT), RSPEOT introduces a residual-driven self-paced update that prioritizes reliable matches and progressively refines harder ones. This design alleviates the additional optimization burden of standard EOT while preserving reliable semantic alignment. Comprehensive experiments under complete and missing-modality protocols show consistent performance across arbitrary modality configurations. Code will be released upon publication.

</details>

TL;DR: 提出一种结合可变形时间对齐与差异感知空间选择性融合的神经视频压缩方法，通过上下文感知时间对齐模块生成互补时间上下文，并利用差异感知空间选择性融合模块自适应选择可靠时间信息、抑制错位，在率失真性能上优于 DCVC-DC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于条件编码的神经视频压缩中，时间上下文质量直接影响压缩性能。现有方法多从传播的参考特征构建上下文，但在复杂运动、遮挡和高频纹理区域容易受到运动估计与局部对齐误差的影响，导致时间信息不准确，从而限制压缩性能。

Method: 提出结合可变形时间对齐与差异感知空间选择性融合的方法，包含两个核心模块：1) 上下文感知时间对齐模块（Context-aware Temporal Alignment Module），用于生成互补的时间上下文；2) 差异感知空间选择性融合模块（Difference-aware Spatial Selective Fusion），自适应地选择可靠的时间信息并抑制不对齐信息。

Result: 在实验中，该方法相较于 DCVC-DC 取得了一定的率失真性能提升，验证了所提模块的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过生成更准确的互补时间上下文并对可靠信息进行自适应选择，所提方法有效缓解了复杂运动、遮挡和高频纹理区域的运动估计与对齐误差问题，显著改善了条件编码下神经视频压缩的性能。

Abstract: In conditional coding-based neural video compression, the quality of temporal context directly affects compression per- formance. Existing methods mostly construct context from prop- agated reference features, but they are vulnerable to motion esti- mation and local alignment errors in regions with complex mo- tion, occlusion, and high-frequency textures, resulting in inaccu- rate temporal information. To address this issue, this paper pro- poses a method combining deformable temporal alignment and difference-aware spatial selective fusion. A Context-aware Tem- poral Alignment Module is used to generate complementary tem- poral context, while a Difference-aware Spatial Selective Fusion module adaptively selects reliable temporal information and sup- presses misalignment. Experiments show that the proposed method achieves certain rate-distortion performance improve- ment over DCVC-DC.

</details>


### [37] [SafeRI: Recognition and Intervention for Token-Level Safety Intervention in Large Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03544)
*Caoyuan Ma,Tian Gu,Wenpu Liu,Weichu Xie,Shuai Dong,Yuqi Xu,Ji Zhao,Ziyue Wang,Wenzheng Chang,Taiqiang Wu,Yongfu Zhu,Wenqi Shao,Zheng Wang,Yinqiang Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向视觉语言模型的按需安全对齐框架，通过轻量级识别器实时判断生成状态的安全性，并动态控制 LoRA 门控开关，仅在检测到不安全生成时激活 LoRA 模块将输出引导回安全回复，避免全局性安全改造对通用能力的损害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 安全对齐方法通常全局性地修改模型行为，安全参数一旦加载便同时参与不安全与已安全的生成过程。这种常开式干预会不必要地扰动模型原有的推理路径，导致通用多模态能力下降。作者主张安全对齐应当是一种按需干预，而非对每条解码轨迹的永久修改。

Method: 提出一种流式识别与门控 LoRA 框架：在自回归生成过程中，一个轻量级识别器估计当前预 token 生成状态是安全还是不安全，其输出更新下一解码步骤的 LoRA 门控；若安全则沿用冻结主干策略，若不安全则激活 LoRA。LoRA 模块由不安全前缀、过渡语句和安全续写训练而成，从而学会将不安全生成重定向回安全回复。

Result: 在多个安全与通用基准上的实验表明，该方法在事后对齐（post-alignment）设置下有效，能在保证安全性的同时减少对通用多模态能力的负面影响。

Conclusion: 安全对齐应作为按需的、条件触发的干预机制而非全局永久改造。通过门控机制按需激活 LoRA，可以在保持模型原始解码路径的同时实现有效的内在安全对齐。

Abstract: Existing safety alignment methods for vision-language models usually modify the model behavior globally: once the safety parameters are trained or loaded, they participate in both unsafe and already-safe generations. This always-on intervention can unnecessarily perturb the model's original reasoning path and degrade general multimodal capabilities. We argue that safety alignment should be an on-demand intervention rather than a permanent modification to every decoding trajectory. To this end, we propose a streaming recognition and gated LoRA framework for intrinsic VLM safety. During autoregressive generation, a lightweight recognizer estimates whether the current pre-token generation state is safe or unsafe. Its output updates the LoRA gate for the following decoding step; otherwise, generation follows the frozen-backbone policy. The LoRA module is trained from unsafe prefixes, transition statements, and safe continuations, so that it learns to redirect unsafe generations back to safe responses after activation. Experiments across multiple safety and general-purpose benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our method in post-alignment settings.

</details>


### [38] [WIDE: Wildcard Inference with Dynamic Expansion for Cross-Modal Generative Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03554)
*Teng Guo,Xin Wang,Jiayou Xu,Keying Zhou,Jifeng Shen,Haoxin Ruan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WIDE（Wildcard Inference with Dynamic Expansion），用于解决跨模态生成式检索中因模态信息不对称导致的“强制幻觉”问题。WIDE通过离线自适应熵阈值（AET）校准不确定性边界，在解码阶段用非对称感知通配符解码（AWD）在语义盲区发射通配符、动态扩展搜索空间以避免对数概率惩罚，并通过盲区重排（BSR）以离散生成置信度与连续语义相似度的混合打分对候选池重排。在M-BEIR基准上超越现有生成式检索方法，在抑制强制幻觉的同时保持紧凑的索引结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式检索在单模态中表现优异，但推广到跨模态检索时，不同模态（如简洁文本查询与稠密视觉候选）之间存在固有信息不对称，导致自回归解码器在标准trie约束束搜索中被迫“强制幻觉”——模型因无法猜到查询中缺失的细粒度细节而受罚，使无关候选抢占排名前列。

Method: 提出WIDE框架，包含三个组件：1）Adaptive Entropy Thresholding (AET)，离线校准各层不确定性边界；2）Asymmetry-aware Wildcard Decoding (AWD)，在解码阶段检测语义盲区并发出通配符而非强制确定标识符，动态扩展搜索空间且不产生log概率惩罚；3）Blind-Spot Re-ranking (BSR)，用离散生成置信度与连续语义相似度的混合打分机制评估扩展后的候选池。

Result: 在M-BEIR基准上的大量实验表明，WIDE优于最先进的生成式检索方法，有效抑制了强制幻觉，同时保持了紧凑的索引结构。

Conclusion: 跨模态生成式检索中的强制幻觉源于模态间信息不对称，通过语义盲区感知的通配符发射与动态搜索空间扩展，可以在不牺牲索引紧凑性的前提下显著提升检索性能。

Abstract: Generative retrieval has demonstrated significant success by unifying representation learning and search into a single sequence-to-sequence generation task. However, extending this paradigm to cross-modal retrieval reveals a critical challenge arising from the inherent information asymmetry across different modalities, such as the gap between concise text queries and dense visual candidates. This structural mismatch causes the autoregressive decoder to suffer from forced hallucination when generating identifiers via standard trie-constrained beam search, where the model is severely penalized for failing to guess fine-grained details absent from the query, allowing irrelevant candidates to hijack top rankings. To address this issue, we propose Wildcard Inference with Dynamic Expansion (WIDE). WIDE employs Adaptive Entropy Thresholding (AET) to calibrate layer-specific uncertainty boundaries offline. During the decoding generation phase, Asymmetry-aware Wildcard Decoding (AWD) detects semantic blind spots and emits wildcards instead of forced deterministic identifiers, dynamically expanding the search space without incurring log-probability penalties. Finally, Blind-Spot Re-ranking (BSR) evaluates the expanded candidate pool using a hybrid scoring mechanism that combines discrete generation confidence with continuous semantic similarity. Extensive experiments on the M-BEIR benchmark demonstrate that WIDE outperforms state-of-the-art generative retrieval methods, effectively suppressing forced hallucination while maintaining compact index structures.

</details>


### [39] [FlashRender: Few-Step Generative Rendering via Camera-Controlled Video MeanFlow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03563)
*Byeongjun Park,Byung-Hoon Kim,Hyungjin Chung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlashRender 是一个少步数（few-step）生成式渲染框架，能够在数秒内将源视频沿目标相机轨迹重渲染。它通过 RETA 对齐源视频表征与冻结视觉几何模型的目标特征，消除采样步数相关的相机控制不一致并降低去噪轨迹曲率；随后用 MeanFlow 目标在低曲率轨迹上微调，并采用 on-policy flow map distillation 修正固定少步采样下的自回放误差，最终以 25 倍更低的采样成本达到与多步基线相当的视频质量和几何一致性，并实现更优的相机可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多步采样的生成式渲染模型存在一个显著问题：相机控制依赖于采样步数（sampling-step-dependent camera control），这是离散化误差的一种明显表现。该不一致性导致去噪轨迹曲率较高，阻碍了后续的步数蒸馏，使得模型难以在少步采样下保持视频质量与几何一致性。

Method: 提出三个互补的组件：（1）RETA（Representation Transformation and Alignment），将源视频的隐式表征与来自冻结视觉几何模型的目标视频特征进行对齐，直接在源视频流内编码几何变换，从而实现与采样步数一致的相机控制、降低去噪轨迹曲率；（2）MeanFlow 目标，在 RETA 诱导的低曲率去噪轨迹上对模型进行微调，以更有效地应对离散化误差；（3）on-policy flow map distillation，用于修正固定少步采样下由自回滚（self-rollout）产生的误差。

Result: 大量实验表明，RETA、MeanFlow 与 on-policy flow map distillation 在少步生成式渲染中发挥互补作用。三者结合使该方法能在 25 倍更低的采样成本下达到与多步基线相当的视频质量与几何一致性，同时在相机可控性上表现更优，即使面对分布外的目标相机轨迹也保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 通过将几何表征对齐、低曲率去噪轨迹微调与 on-policy 流图蒸馏相结合，FlashRender 首次实现了秒级、少步数的生成式渲染，在采样效率、视频质量、几何一致性和相机可控性之间取得了更好的权衡，为该方向提供了可行的少步训练范式。

Abstract: We present FlashRender, a few-step generative rendering framework that retakes a source video along a target camera trajectory in seconds. We identify sampling-step-dependent camera control as a prominent manifestation of discretization error in existing multi-step generative rendering models and show that resolving this inconsistency substantially lowers denoising trajectory curvature, facilitating subsequent step distillation. To this end, we introduce Representation Transformation and Alignment (RETA), which aligns hidden source-video representations with target-video features from a frozen visual geometry model. This directly encodes the geometric transformation within the source-video stream, enabling sampling-step-consistent camera control. We then fine-tune the model with the MeanFlow objective on the lower-curvature denoising trajectory induced by RETA, allowing the model to more effectively address discretization error. Finally, we apply on-policy flow map distillation to correct self-rollout errors under fixed few-step sampling. Extensive experiments show that RETA, MeanFlow, and on-policy flow map distillation play complementary roles in few-step generative rendering. Together, they enable our approach to match multi-step baselines in video quality and geometric consistency at 25x lower sampling cost while achieving superior camera controllability, even under out-of-distribution target camera trajectories.

</details>


### [40] [Occlusion-Robust Multimodal Emotion Recognition in VR via Fusion of Facial Images and EMG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03569)
*Birgit Nierula,Karam Tomotaki-Dawoud,Mert Akguel,Mustafa Tevfik Lafci,David Przewozny,Anna Hilsmann,Peter Eisert,Sebastian Bosse*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合下脸视频与上脸肌电信号(EMG)的多模态情感识别方法，以解决VR头戴显示器遮挡上脸导致传统图像表情分析失效的问题，在7类情感分类任务上达到51%的宏F1，优于单一模态基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头戴式显示器(HMD)遮挡了使用者上脸区域，使得基于图像的常规面部表情识别在VR环境中信息不完整，尤其在需要实时情感评估的应用中受限，亟需一种能适应HMD遮挡条件的情感识别方案。

Method: 构建由20名参与者组成的同步多模态数据集，将下脸视频与七通道上脸EMG配对，并使用经过验证的情感刺激诱发情感；提出一种晚期融合架构，将卷积视觉嵌入与基于RBF核的EMG表示相结合，在受试者独立测试下对七类情感（六种基本情绪加中性）进行分类。

Result: 所提出的晚期融合模型在受试者独立测试下达到51%的宏F1值，优于图像单模态(41%)和EMG单模态(43%)基线，证明上脸EMG在HMD视觉遮挡下能提供稳健的互补信息。

Conclusion: 上脸EMG与下脸视频的多模态融合可有效弥补HMD造成的面部信息缺失，为自然VR环境下的多模态情感识别奠定基础，并可支撑情感自适应应用（如沟通训练与治疗干预）；数据集将在伦理使用协议下按需共享。

Abstract: Head-mounted displays (HMDs) fundamentally limit emotion recognition in virtual reality (VR): by occluding the upper face, they render conventional image-based facial expression analysis incomplete, particularly for applications requiring real-time affective assessment. We address this challenge by fusing lower-face video with facial electromyography (EMG) from the occluded upper face to classify seven emotional categories (six basic emotions plus neutral). We introduce a synchronized multimodal dataset from 20 participants, pairing lower-face video with seven-channel upper-face EMG elicited by validated emotion stimuli. Under subject-independent test, our proposed late-fusion architecture merging convolutional visual embeddings with RBF-kernel EMG representations achieves 51% macro-F1, outperforming both image-only (41%) and EMG-only (43%) baselines. These results demonstrate that upper-face EMG provides robust complementary information under HMD-induced visual occlusion and establish a foundation for multimodal emotion recognition in naturalistic VR environments. This approach facilitates affect-adaptive applications, including communication training and therapeutic interventions. The dataset will be shared upon request under an ethical-use agreement.

</details>


### [41] [Drive-HWM: Hierarchical World Models for Dynamic-Latent Guided Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03572)
*Zhaoxin Fan,Tianbao Zhang,Wenjun Wu,Xiaofeng Wang,Yeying Jin,Jian Zhao,Zheng Zhu,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Drive-HWM 提出一种分层慢-快世界建模框架，通过慢世界模型预测多步未来表征以捕获长期场景演化，并由快模型基于最新观测联合预测下一帧与即时动作，实现长期预判与响应式决策的统一，在 NAVSIM v1/v2 上取得优异驾驶性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶世界模型要么将未来预测与动作生成分离，要么在相同时间尺度上联合预测二者，难以同时兼顾长期视野的预判能力与基于观测的快速响应决策能力。

Method: 构建分层"慢-快"世界建模框架：慢世界模型在多步时间尺度上预测未来表征以刻画场景扩展演化，并引入通过光流预测学习的 Dynamic-Aware Latents 显式建模驾驶环境中丰富的运动动态；快模型则在慢模型未来表征的引导下，使用轻量级多模态主干和自回归专家，联合预测下一帧和由最新观测驱动的即时动作，从而既捕捉即将发生的场景演化，又能随新观测持续更新决策。

Result: 在 NAVSIM v1 与 v2 上的大量实验表明 Drive-HWM 具有强有力的驾驶性能；全面消融实验验证了分层慢-快设计、动态感知未来表征以及联合下一帧与动作预测三个组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 分层慢-快世界建模能有效协调长期预判与即时响应，动态感知的未来表征显著提升运动建模能力，联合下一帧与动作预测是提升自动驾驶决策性能的关键设计。

Abstract: World models offer a promising paradigm for autonomous driving by predicting how traffic scenes may evolve and using such predictions to support action generation. However, existing approaches either separate future prediction from action generation or jointly predict them at the same temporal scale, making it difficult to simultaneously achieve long-horizon anticipation and responsive, observation-grounded decision making. We present Drive-HWM, a hierarchical slow--fast world modeling framework that organizes future representation prediction and action generation at complementary temporal scales. The slow world model predicts multi-step future representations to capture extended scene evolution. To explicitly model the abundant motion dynamics in driving environments, we introduce Dynamic-Aware Latents learned through optical-flow prediction. Guided by these future representations, the fast model uses a lightweight multimodal backbone and an autoregressive expert to jointly predict the next frame and the immediate action from the latest observation. Next-frame prediction encourages the fast model to capture imminent scene evolution, while one-step action generation allows decisions to be continuously updated as new observations arrive. Extensive experiments on NAVSIM v1 and v2 demonstrate the strong driving performance of Drive-HWM. Comprehensive ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of the hierarchical slow--fast design, dynamics-aware future representations, and joint next-frame and action prediction.

</details>


### [42] [Text2Thermal: Physics-Aware Thermal Image Synthesis from Textual Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03585)
*Tayeba Qazi,Brejesh Lall,Prerana Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Text2Thermal，一种基于文本先验的物理感知热红外图像合成框架，通过热学语义描述（材料、天气、时段、发热状态）显式给定辐射度量信息，并微调预训练 Stable Diffusion 至热红外域，从而无需配准 RGB 图像即可合成高质量热像，且支持文本级可控生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热红外成像在黑暗与恶劣天气下可靠，但热红外数据集稀缺，促使大量研究将丰富的 RGB 图像迁移到热红外域。然而这种迁移本质上是病态的：热外观由表面发射率和物体温度决定，而这两者在可见光谱中均不可观测，因此单个 RGB 图像对应多种合理的热输出。作者认为语言是消解这种歧义的自然手段。

Method: 提出 Text2Thermal 框架：不尝试从 RGB 推断不可观测的辐射度量因素，而是通过编码材料、天气、时段和发热状态的热学接地文本描述显式提供这些信息；将预训练的 Stable Diffusion 主干适配到热红外域。由于辐射度量内容完全由提示词决定，推理时无需配准 RGB 图像；如需空间引导，可选用控制信号注入场景几何，同时不干扰提示词指定的辐射度量。

Result: 在 M3FD、FLIR 和 FMB 数据集上的实验表明，Text2Thermal 在热图像合成方法中取得了最优的 FID，同时提供基于翻译的方法无法实现的文本级控制能力。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了以语言为媒介显式提供辐射度量先验能够有效解决 RGB 到热红外迁移的病态歧义问题，实现了免配准、物理可解释且文本可控的热红外图像合成，为热红外数据稀缺问题提供了一种新的解决范式。

Abstract: Thermal infrared imaging offers reliable perception in darkness and adverse weather, but thermal datasets remain scarce, motivating extensive work on translating abundant RGB images into thermal. Such translation is fundamentally ill-posed as thermal appearance is governed by surface emissivity and object temperature, neither of which is observable in the visible spectrum, so a single RGB image is consistent with many valid thermal outputs. We argue that language offers a natural means of resolving this ambiguity, and propose Text2Thermal, a framework for physics-aware thermal image synthesis from textual priors. Rather than inferring the unobservable radiometric factors from RGB, we supply them explicitly through thermally grounded captions encoding material, weather, time-of-day, and heat-emission state, and adapt a pretrained Stable Diffusion backbone to the thermal domain. Because the radiometric content is determined entirely by the prompt, Text2Thermal synthesizes thermal imagery without requiring a registered RGB image at inference; where spatial guidance is desired, an optional control signal imparts scene geometry without disturbing the prompt-specified radiometry. Experiments on M3FD, FLIR, and FMB show that Text2Thermal achieves state-of-the-art FID among thermal image synthesis methods while offering text-level control that translation-based approaches cannot provide.

</details>


### [43] [SV-WAM: An Efficient Surround-View World-Action Model for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03602)
*Jinyang Wang,Shiwei Li,Junjian Wang,Zhiqiang Deng,Jianbin Gao,Yihang Zhao,Liu Liu,Yongjia Zhao,Jinlong Chen,Huirui Xu,Yifeng Pan,Kangwei Liu,Fan Ren,Ji Tao,Minghao Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SV-WAM 提出一种保留六相机环视观测的世界-动作模型（WAM），将未来视频预测仅作为训练阶段的稠密监督（而非推理输出），通过动作中心因果掩码实现动作与视频的联合去噪，部署时可丢弃视频分支，从而在保持高效推理的同时获得大范围空间感知；并引入可微的可行驶区域合规正则化提升规划安全性与边界意识，在 NAVSIMv2 与 nuScenes 基准上达到 SOTA 规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型虽能通过学习未来场景动态实现端到端自动驾驶，但推理时生成未来视频计算开销巨大，因此许多近期驾驶世界模型改用单前视相机以换取高效部署；这严重限制了换道、汇入和转弯等安全关键场景的空间覆盖范围。为此需要一种既能保留全六相机环视信息、又保持高效推理的建模方案。

Method: 提出 SV-WAM：在同一共享生成模型中，将未来视频预测用作动作学习的稠密训练监督（而非推理期输出）；核心是动作中心因果掩码，在联合动作-视频去噪过程中阻止动作 token 注意到未来视频 token，从而使部署时可完全丢弃视频分支、仅保留高效的动作规划。此外引入可微的可行驶区域合规正则化，对车辆足迹角点接近或越过可行驶区域边界的行为施加惩罚，以提升规划安全性和边界感知。

Result: 在闭环 NAVSIMv2 基准与开环 nuScenes 基准上的大量实验表明，SV-WAM 在低推理延迟下取得最先进的规划性能，并具备具有竞争力的零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: SV-WAM 证明可以通过"训练时用视频监督、推理时用纯动作"的设计，兼顾世界模型的监督能力与部署效率，在保留六相机环视输入的情况下实现高效、安全的自动驾驶规划，为世界模型驱动驾驶提供了新的实用范式。

Abstract: World models (WMs) have demonstrated strong potential for end-to-end autonomous driving by learning predictive representations of future scene dynamics. However, generating future videos during inference introduces substantial computational overhead, leading many recent driving WMs to adopt a single front camera as input for efficient deployment. This design restricts spatial coverage in safety-critical maneuvers such as lane changes, merges, and turns. To address this limitation, we propose SV-WAM, a surround-view world-action model (WAM) that preserves full six-camera observations while maintaining efficient inference. SV-WAM leverages future-video prediction as dense training supervision for action learning within a shared generative model, rather than as an inference-time output. At the core of this design is an action-centered causal mask that prevents action tokens from attending to future-video tokens during joint action-video denoising. Consequently, the video branch can be discarded at deployment, enabling efficient action-only planning. Furthermore, we introduce a differentiable drivable-area compliance regularizer that penalizes vehicle-footprint corners approaching or crossing drivable boundaries, improving planning safety and boundary awareness. Extensive experiments on the closed-loop NAVSIMv2 benchmark and the open-loop nuScenes benchmark demonstrate that SV-WAM achieves state-of-the-art planning performance with low inference latency and competitive zero-shot transfer capability.

</details>


### [44] [Auditing Patient Privacy in Medical Generative Models: Scalable Memorization Detection with DeepSSIM++](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03615)
*Antonio Scardace,Francesco Guarnera,Sebastiano Battiato,Daniele Ravì*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepSSIM++，一种用于医学生成模型记忆化审计的自监督相似度度量。它通过多尺度特征聚合与解剖保持增强，学习一个余弦相似度逼近SSIM的嵌入空间，无需像素级精确配准，在宏F1上比现有基线分别提升33个百分点（理想对齐）和46个百分点（实际扰动下），且大规模相似度计算比解析SSIM快若干数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度生成模型虽为医学图像合成与数据共享带来新机会，但其记忆并复现训练样本的能力引发患者保密性方面的严重担忧。现有检测手段存在局限：基于像素的度量对生成伪影敏感，而通用基于嵌入的度量又缺乏医学数据所需的解剖敏感性，因此在大规模场景下检测记忆化仍然困难。

Method: DeepSSIM++采用自监督学习，利用多尺度特征聚合与解剖保持的增强策略，学习一个嵌入空间，使其中余弦相似度能够逼近结构相似性指数（SSIM），从而免去精确的像素级配准要求。

Result: 与最先进的基线相比，DeepSSIM++在理想对齐条件下平均宏F1提升33个百分点，在实际空间与强度扰动条件下提升46个百分点；同时，与解析SSIM相比，其大规模相似度计算加速达若干数量级。

Conclusion: DeepSSIM++兼顾解剖敏感性与计算效率，为医学生成式AI提供了可扩展的记忆化审计开源工具，代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: While deep generative models offer new opportunities for medical image synthesis and data sharing, their ability to memorize and reproduce training samples raises serious concerns about patient confidentiality. Detecting such memorization at scale remains challenging: traditional pixel-based metrics are sensitive to generation artifacts, whereas generic embedding-based metrics often lack the anatomical sensitivity required for medical data. To address this challenge, we introduce DeepSSIM++, a self-supervised similarity metric for scalable memorization auditing in medical generative models. By leveraging multi-scale feature aggregation and anatomy-preserving augmentations, DeepSSIM++ learns an embedding space where cosine similarity approximates the Structural Similarity Index (SSIM), eliminating the need for exact pixel-level registration. Compared with state-of-the-art baselines, DeepSSIM++ achieves an average Macro F1 improvement of 33 percentage points under ideal alignment and 46 percentage points under realistic spatial and intensity perturbations. Furthermore, it accelerates large-scale similarity computation by several orders of magnitude compared with analytical SSIM. By combining anatomical sensitivity and computational efficiency, DeepSSIM++ provides an open-source tool for scalable memorization auditing in medical generative AI. Code and data are publicly available at: https://github.com/brAIn-science/DeepSSIM.

</details>


### [45] [EraseSAE: Surgical Concept Erasure in Text-to-Video Diffusion Models via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03629)
*Xinghao Wang,Dong Li,Wei Yu,Yingwei Pan,Tao Gong,Qi Chu,Nenghai Yu,Ting Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EraseSAE 框架，利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）在基于 DiT 的文生视频扩散模型上实现细粒度（单语义特征级别）的概念擦除。其通过"分解-归因-擦除"流水线：先用分区卷积稀疏自编码器将稠密时空激活分解为可解释的稀疏特征，再用对比归因机制从配对提示中定位概念专属特征核，最后用基于时间步的时空掩码将擦除限制在目标概念活跃区域，从而实现精准、鲁棒且几乎不损伤生成质量的概念移除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生视频扩散模型因依赖松散的训练数据而带来安全与版权隐患，概念擦除是原则性解决方案。但现有方法在过于粗粒度的层面操作，与概念表示"细粒度、分布式"的本质不匹配，导致概念移除不彻底或生成质量退化。作者主张外科手术式擦除必须在单语义特征层面（每个单元编码单一可解释概念）干预。

Method: 提出 EraseSAE 框架，包含三条核心组件：(1) 分区卷积稀疏自编码器（Partitioned Convolutional Sparse Autoencoder），将稠密时空激活分解为解纠缠、可解释的稀疏特征，同时保持时空连贯性；(2) 对比归因机制，通过对比配对提示的激活来隔离概念专属特征核；(3) 推理阶段基于时间步解析的时空掩码，将擦除限定在目标概念活跃区域，其余内容不受影响。整体构成 decompose-attribute-erase 流水线。

Result: 在多种扩散模型和概念擦除任务上的大量实验表明，EraseSAE 实现了精准且鲁棒的概念移除，生成质量退化极小，显著优于现有最先进方法。代码已开源。

Conclusion: EraseSAE 验证了在单语义特征层面干预是实现精准、低损伤概念擦除的关键，为 DiT 类文生视频扩散模型的安全对齐与版权治理提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in text-to-video (T2V) diffusion models have demonstrated remarkable generative capabilities, yet their reliance on loosely curated training data raises pressing safety and copyright concerns. Concept erasure offers a principled remedy by removing unwanted semantics from pretrained models while preserving remaining concepts. However, existing approaches typically operate at a coarse granularity misaligned with the fine-grained, distributed nature of concept representations, leading to incomplete removal or degraded generation quality. We argue that surgical erasure fundamentally requires intervention at the level of monosemantic features, where each unit encodes a single interpretable concept. To this end, we propose EraseSAE, a novel framework that leverages sparse autoencoders to achieve surgical concept erasure in DiT-based T2V diffusion models via a principled decompose-attribute-erase pipeline. We first introduce the Partitioned Convolutional Sparse Autoencoder, which decomposes dense spatiotemporal activations into disentangled, interpretable sparse features while preserving spatiotemporal coherence. A contrastive attribution mechanism then contrasts activations from paired prompts to isolate concept-specific feature kernels. At inference, timestep-resolved spatiotemporal masks derived from the identified kernels confine erasure to regions where the target concept is active, leaving unrelated content intact. Extensive experiments across diverse diffusion models and concept erasure tasks demonstrate that EraseSAE achieves precise and robust concept removal with minimal quality degradation, substantially outperforming state-of-the-art methods. The code is available at https://github.com/HiDream-ai/EraseSAE.

</details>


### [46] [Stabilizing Camera-Controlled Novel View Synthesis at Inference Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03639)
*Prajwal Singh,Arjun Badola,Seema Kumari,Hajime Nagahara,Shanmuganathan Raman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文（CamTrol++）针对基于预训练视频扩散模型的免训练、相机可控新视角合成在大相机运动与长序列生成下不稳定的问题，发现稳定性的关键在于将相机运动分解为小步长自回归步骤，并佐以几何约束的空间注意力与低频外观锚定等改进，从而在不重训、不改动扩散骨干的前提下显著提升时间与几何一致性及生成效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练的相机可控新视角合成在面临大相机运动和长时程生成时往往不稳定；已有方法通常会组合多种推理期组件，但究竟哪些设计选择对稳定性最重要并不明确，缺乏对稳定性来源的清晰归因。

Method: 将相机运动分解为小的自回归步长以限制每步几何畸变并减少误差累积；通过受控的相机步长实验（发现步长接近18-20°时性能显著退化）验证该核心策略；并进一步评估几何约束的空间注意力、低频外观锚定以及一种免配准的高效warping流水线作为辅助增强。

Result: 在RealEstate10K和MegaScene数据集上，CamTrol++相比免训练基线在时间一致性、几何一致性、下游三维重建质量以及生成效率上均取得提升；该方法在56帧生成以及受控深度严重损坏条件下依然有效。

Conclusion: 推理期对相机运动的精细控制能在无需重训或修改扩散骨干的前提下大幅提升相机可控新视角合成的稳定性；小步长自回归的相机分解是稳定性的主要来源，而辅助组件提供进一步支撑。

Abstract: Training-free, camera-controlled novel view synthesis from a single image using pre-trained video diffusion models often becomes unstable under large camera motion and long generation horizons. Existing approaches commonly combine several inference-time components, making it unclear which design choices are most important for stability. We show that the main source of stability is simple. Decomposing camera motion into small autoregressive steps limits per-step geometric distortion and reduces error accumulation. A controlled camera-step study shows that performance remains stable for small motions and degrades more strongly as the per-step motion approaches $18$-$20^\circ$. We further evaluate geometry-constrained spatial attention and low-frequency appearance anchoring as supporting refinements, together with an efficient registration-free warping pipeline. Across RealEstate10K and MegaScene, CamTrol++ improves temporal and geometric consistency, downstream 3D reconstruction quality, and generation efficiency over training-free baselines. The method remains effective for 56-frame generation and under substantial controlled depth corruption. These results show that careful control of camera motion at inference time can substantially improve the stability of camera-controlled novel view synthesis without retraining or modifying the diffusion backbone.

</details>


### [47] [Tree-Structured Vector Quantization For Efficient And Progressive Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03641)
*Xinkun Wang,Tianyi Xu,Qingyu Luo,Mingming Ma,Changzhe Jiao,Fu Li,Yi Niu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Tree-VQ 是一种渐进式树结构向量量化（VQ）框架，将离散码字组织成层次化二叉树，使得每个潜在 token 对应一条从根到叶的路径；路径的任何前缀都是有效可解码的量化表示，从而让压缩图像可从早期前缀解码并通过追加比特逐步细化，实现变比特率与渐进式比特流的一体化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于向量量化的图像压缩方法虽具备强率失真性能，但大多数仍需为每个目标码率生成独立的压缩表示；这类可变码率方法虽能用单一模型适配多个码率，却不能提供渐进式比特流——即前缀本身可解码、且可通过追加比特逐步细化的比特流。作者旨在构建一种同时支持多码率与渐进式解码的统一框架。

Method: Tree-VQ 将离散码字组织为层次化二叉树，用从根到叶的路由路径表示每个潜在 token，且路径的每个前缀都是合法量化表示：浅层内部节点充当粗重建码，深层节点提供逐级细化。为使该结构实用化，作者提出：1) 前缀兼容的树熵模型，仅利用因果可用的解码上下文对渐进延续决策与分支细化进行熵编码；2) 率感知细化调度，在给定前缀预算下决定哪些空间块应获得额外树比特；3) 层次化前缀监督，确保内部节点在低码率下可直接解码。

Result: 实验表明 Tree-VQ 实现了优异的性能-效率权衡，以更少的参数和更低的延迟取得了最好的感知压缩效果，优于同类竞争方法，同时支持真正的渐进式解码。

Conclusion: Tree-VQ 证明借助树结构向量量化与因果熵建模，可以在单一模型中同时获得变码率能力和前缀可解码的渐进式比特流，为学习型图像压缩提供了高效且实用的新方案。

Abstract: Vector-quantization based image compression has achieved strong rate--distortion performance, yet most of them still produce a separate compressed representation for each target bitrate. Such variable-rate behavior allows one model to operate at multiple rates, but it does not necessarily provide a progressive bitstream whose prefixes are themselves decodable and can be refined by appending additional bits. We propose \textbf{Tree-VQ}, a progressive tree-structured vector quantization framework for learned image compression. Tree-VQ organizes discrete codewords as a hierarchical binary tree and represents each latent token by a routed root-to-leaf path. Crucially, every prefix of this path corresponds to a valid quantized representation, so shallow internal nodes serve as coarse reconstruction codes and deeper nodes provide successive refinements. This allows a compressed image to be decoded from an early prefix and progressively improved as more branch symbols are received, rather than being re-encoded for different target rates. To make this structure practical for compression, we introduce a prefix-compatible tree entropy model that codes progressive continuation decisions and routed branch refinements using only causally available decoded contexts. We further use rate-aware refinement scheduling to decide which spatial blocks should receive additional tree bits under a given prefix budget, and hierarchical prefix supervision to ensure that internal nodes are directly decodable at low rates. Experiments show that Tree-VQ achieves a superior performance--efficiency trade-off, delivering the best perceptual compression results with much fewer parameters and lower latency than competing methods.

</details>


### [48] [PL-SCEA: Reconfiguring Pretrained Attention for Few-Shot Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03655)
*Xiaoyu Yang,Qixing Wu,Huixian Zhao,Changlong Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 PL-SCEA（幂律自相关增强注意力），将冻结的视觉基础模型（VFM）的注意力计算重配置为面向工业异常检测的任务相关组件，并通过轻量级 VAE 进行重建式正常性建模，在 MVTec AD 和 VisA 上实现具有竞争力的少样本异常检测与稳定的像素级定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型提供的 patch 级表示虽具有迁移性，但其注意力计算源自以语义聚合为核心的预训练目标，导致 token 关系更侧重语义识别，而难以暴露异常定位所需的局部纹理与结构偏差。作者由此质疑：能否将冻结 VFM 的注意力计算重配置为服务于异常检测的任务相关组件。

Method: 提出 PL-SCEA：保留预训练 query-key 注意力的语义上下文，同时对上下文化的 value 特征构建 token 自适应的自相关；通过正相关过滤和幂律重加权，强调相对各 token 关系背景更显著的关系，且不引入额外可训练注意力投影。所得特征由轻量级变分自编码器（VAE）建模，生成固定大小的类别特定正常性重建表示。两阶段互补：注意力重配置塑造局部关系偏差的表示方式，重建建模将偏差转化为异常分数。

Result: 在 MVTec AD 和 VisA 数据集上，完整框架在图像级检测上达到具有竞争力的性能，并在所评估的少样本设置下持续获得较强的像素级定位效果。消融实验表明，在测试设置下，无论搭配 VAE 还是记忆库，PL-SCEA 均能改善定位性能。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持一个观点：任务对齐的注意力重配置能够提升冻结预训练表示的异常定位能力，即注意力机制不应被视为不可变的预训练产物，而可作为异常检测中可适配的关键组件。

Abstract: Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) provide transferable patch representations for few-shot industrial anomaly detection, but their attention computation is typically inherited from pretraining objectives centered on semantic aggregation. This creates a potential mismatch: token relations that support semantic recognition may not adequately expose the localized texture and structural deviations required for anomaly localization. We therefore investigate the hypothesis that the attention computation of a frozen VFM can be reconfigured as a task-relevant component of anomaly detection. We instantiate this idea with Power-Law Self-Correlation Enhanced Attention (PL-SCEA), which retains the semantic context of pretrained query-key attention while constructing token-adaptive self-correlations over contextualized value features. Positive-correlation filtering and power-law reweighting then emphasize relations that are salient relative to each token's relational background, without introducing additional trainable attention projections. The resulting features are modeled by a lightweight variational autoencoder that provides a fixed-size reconstruction-based representation of category-specific normality. The two stages serve complementary roles: attention reconfiguration shapes how local relational deviations are represented, while reconstruction-based modeling converts deviations from learned normality into anomaly scores. Across MVTec AD and VisA, the complete framework achieves competitive image-level detection and consistently strong pixel-level localization across the evaluated few-shot settings. Ablations further show that PL-SCEA improves localization with either the VAE or a memory bank under the tested setting. These results support the view that task-aligned attention reconfiguration can improve the anomaly-localization capability of frozen pretrained representations.

</details>


### [49] [Rethinking 3D Noise: Learning 3D-Aware Video Priors via Optimization-Free Morphological Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03657)
*Onat Şahin,Mohammad Altillawi,George Eskandar,Carlos Carbone,Ziyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种免优化的3D形态扰动正则化方法，将每个3D高斯视为类似2D像素的基本构件，通过在尺度、旋转和剪枝等形态参数空间中施加扰动，在不进行逐场景重建的情况下保持跨视角空间一致性，用于稀疏视角下3D场景表示（NeRF/3DGS）的训练增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NeRF和3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）在稀疏视角设置下会产生严重伪影。现有的生成式3D伪影修复器依赖成对的损坏与干净渲染，需要针对不同视角配置进行昂贵的逐场景重建。而2D图像增强虽可作为即时正则化手段，但3D表示缺乏等价的正则化方法来保持跨视角空间一致性，这是3D感知训练的关键属性。

Method: 提出3D形态扰动（3D Morphological Perturbations）作为免优化且保持空间一致性的正则化器。利用显式3DGS表示，将每个高斯视为类似2D像素的基本构建块，在其形态参数空间中通过尺度（scale）、旋转（rotation）和剪枝（pruning）施加扰动。该方法消除了数据集构建中的逐场景3DGS优化循环，并通过ControlNet扩展到14B参数的视频模型。

Result: 在轻量级视频扩散实验台上，该方法使模型学习到比稀疏视角基线更强的几何先验；扩展到14B参数视频模型后，在保持视觉保真度的同时，将平均深度误差较最先进的图像到图像3D伪影修复器降低12.5%，并在4个操作任务中的3个上将下游机器人策略成功率提升最高8.0%。

Conclusion: 所提出的3D形态扰动作为一种简单、免优化且保持空间一致性的正则化手段，可替代昂贵的逐场景重建式数据增强，有效提升稀疏视角下3D感知模型的几何先验学习能力，并在大规模视频模型及下游机器人操作任务中展现出显著的性能提升。

Abstract: 3D scene representations like NeRF and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) suffer severe artifacts in sparse-view settings. Recent generative 3D artifact fixers attempt to address this, but rely on paired corrupted and clean renders requiring costly, per-scene reconstructions across varying view configurations. While 2D image augmentations act as instant regularizers, no explicit equivalents exist for 3D representations to preserve spatial consistency across views, an essential property for 3D-aware training. We propose 3D Morphological Perturbations as an optimization-free regularizer that preserves spatial consistency. Leveraging explicit 3DGS, we treat each Gaussian as a fundamental building block - analogous to a 2D pixel - and apply perturbations across its morphological parameter space via scale, rotation, and pruning. Our method eliminates per-scene 3DGS optimization loops from dataset curation while enabling models to learn stronger geometric priors than sparse-view baselines in diagnostic ablations conducted on a lightweight video diffusion sandbox. Scaled to a 14B-parameter video model via ControlNet, our approach maintains visual fidelity while reducing mean depth error by 12.5% over state-of-the-art image-to-image 3D artifact refiners, ultimately boosting downstream robotics policy success rates by up to 8.0% across 3 of 4 manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [50] [Cross-Dataset Transfer and Reliability of Explainable Artificial Intelligence for RhythmFormer Remote Photoplethysmography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03663)
*Louis Chen,Torbjörn E. M. Nordling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文系统量化了八个条件特异性RhythmFormer模型在远程光电容积描记（rPPG）任务中的注意力归属解释，发现Beyond Intuition方法在皮肤覆盖度与Salience-guided Faithfulness Coefficient（SaCo）上均优于原始注意力、rollout和attention flow，但解释指标与单条片段的预测误差、波形相关性和信噪比几乎无关联，且跨场景时仅Beyond Intuition的覆盖度与三种性能指标具有一定相关性。结论认为皮肤覆盖度和SaCo提供的是与性能互补的信息，而非性能的代理指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程光电容积描记（rPPG）从面部视频估计心血管脉搏，以往对该方法的解释依赖人工查看热力图，缺乏关于模型究竟从哪里读取信号的定量证据。本文旨在量化这些解释，并考察解释是否能在数据集间迁移、以及是否追踪模型性能的变化。

Method: 训练了八个条件特异性的RhythmFormer模型，数据集为NCKU-rPPG，涵盖三种光照水平、说话、旋转和骑行条件，每个5.12秒片段估计一次心率，并与UBFC-rPPG上的复现模型并列比较。评估了原始注意力、rollout、attention flow以及Beyond Intuition四种解释方法，使用皮肤覆盖度（skin coverage）和Salience-guided Faithfulness Coefficient（SaCo）进行量化评估。

Result: Beyond Intuition在两个数据集上均排名最高（Static level 3的中位覆盖度0.789、SaCo 0.837；UBFC-rPPG上为0.826和0.917），其余方法排名不同。在单一参与者的单一条件下，两个指标与片段心率误差、波形相关性或信噪比均无关系：252个系数中有186个低于|ρ|=0.10，仅28个达到p<0.05（随机期望为13个）。跨八个场景时仅Beyond Intuition的覆盖度与三项性能指标相关（ρ=-0.43、+0.57、+0.43），而仅注意力方法（attention-only）的SaCo与性能相反。在40 lux光照下该指标单独失效，中位覆盖度降至0.180、中位SaCo降至-0.178，而运动对估计的破坏更大却未出现这种下降。

Conclusion: 皮肤覆盖度和SaCo携带的信息与性能指标互补，而非作为其代理：归属于皮肤并不能保证估计准确。归属解释对某一条件所揭示的，是模型关注的位置，而不是其注意力图排序的忠实程度。

Abstract: Background. Remote photoplethysmography estimates the cardiovascular pulse from facial video, and its explanations have rested on inspecting heatmaps rather than on quantitative evidence about where a model reads it. We quantified the explanations and asked whether such explanations transfer between datasets and track model performance. Method. We trained eight condition-specific RhythmFormer models on NCKU-rPPG, recorded under three illumination levels, speaking, rotation, and cycling, estimated one heart rate per 5.12-second clip, and set them beside a UBFC-rPPG reproduction. Raw attention, rollout, attention flow, and Beyond Intuition were assessed by skin coverage and the Salience-guided Faithfulness Coefficient (SaCo). Results. Beyond Intuition ranked highest on both datasets, at median coverage 0.789 and SaCo 0.837 on Static level 3 against 0.826 and 0.917 on UBFC-rPPG; lower ranks differed. Within one participant of one condition, neither measure was related to a clip's heart-rate error, waveform correlation, or signal-to-noise ratio on either dataset: 186 of the 252 coefficients fell below $|ρ|=0.10$ and 28 reached $p<0.05$ against the 13 expected by chance. Across the eight scenarios only Beyond Intuition's coverage followed the three performance measures, at $ρ=-0.43$, $+0.57$, and $+0.43$, while the attention-only methods' SaCo ran opposite to each. It failed at 40 lux alone, its median coverage falling to 0.180 and its median SaCo to $-0.178$, whereas motion degraded the estimates far more without such a drop. Conclusions. Skin coverage and SaCo carry information complementary to the performance measures rather than a proxy for them: attributing to the skin does not guarantee an accurate estimate. What an attribution reveals about a condition is where the model looks rather than how faithfully its map is ordered.

</details>


### [51] [ARCOS: Zero-shot Boundary Localization for Corneal Layer Segmentation Across Optical Coherence Tomography Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03668)
*Nuno Vivas Brás,Benjamin Memmi,Maëlle Bouhassane,Cristina Georgeon,Vincent Borderie,Karsten Plamann,Anatole Chessel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ARCOS，一种基于图像块的零样本边界定位框架，用于眼前节OCT图像的角膜分层分割。该方法不采用传统的区域分类，而是从重叠的原生分辨率图像块预测角膜主要界面的边界热图，拼接后转换为连续、解剖有序的分层。网络结合多尺度特征融合与自条件细化模块，在多种OCT设备和零样本跨设备设置下优于现有基线，达到95.1%的off-by-one边界定位准确率和0.514像素的平均绝对边界误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 角膜OCT的分层分割对量化角膜形态（层厚、疾病或手术相关结构变化）至关重要，但自动分割面临角膜界面薄、受散斑噪声影响、以及不同采集设备间差异大等挑战，因此需要一种鲁棒且可跨设备泛化的分割方法。

Method: 提出ARCOS框架：采用基于重叠原生分辨率图像块的零样本边界定位方式预测角膜主要界面的边界热图；将图像块级预测拼接至整个B-scan并转换为边界位置，得到连续且解剖有序的分层；网络结合多尺度特征融合与自条件细化模块，后者利用中间边界信息改进局部热图预测并保持空间细节。

Result: 在匹配设备测试集上实现off-by-one边界定位准确率95.1%、平均绝对边界误差0.514像素；在零样本跨设备评估中，对未见设备保持平均off-by-one准确率84.3%与平均绝对边界误差0.855像素，优于各基线模型；由预测边界导出的厚度估计在各角膜区域误差较低。

Conclusion: ARCOS以零样本方式在多种OCT设备上实现了准确、鲁棒的角膜边界定位与分层分割，得到的厚度指标误差小，可支持定量角膜OCT分析。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of corneal layers in optical coherence tomography (OCT) is essential for quantitative assessment of corneal morphology, including layer thickness and structural changes associated with disease or surgery. However, automatic segmentation remains challenging because corneal interfaces are thin, affected by speckle noise, and variable across acquisition devices. In this work, we propose ARCOS, a patch-based zero-shot boundary localization framework for corneal layer segmentation in clinical anterior-segment OCT images. Rather than performing conventional region classification, the method predicts boundary heatmaps for the main corneal interfaces from overlapping native-resolution patches. Patch-level predictions are stitched across the full B-scan and converted into boundary locations to obtain continuous, anatomically ordered layer segmentations. The network combines multi-scale feature fusion with a self-conditioned refinement module that uses intermediate boundary information to improve local heatmap predictions while preserving spatial detail. The method was evaluated on clinical OCT images acquired from multiple devices and compared with representative segmentation baselines using boundary localization and derived thickness metrics. The proposed method achieved an off-by-one boundary localization accuracy of 95.1% and a mean absolute boundary error of 0.514 pixels on the matched-device test set. In zero-shot cross-device evaluation, it maintained an average off-by-one accuracy of 84.3% and a mean absolute boundary error of 0.855 pixels across unseen acquisition devices, outperforming the baseline models. Thickness estimates derived from the predicted boundaries showed low error across corneal regions, supporting the method's use for quantitative corneal OCT analysis.

</details>


### [52] [Do Video Generators Track the World Across Segments? A Benchmark and Method for World-State Reasoning in Video Continuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03673)
*Yingmao Miao,Pengfei Zhang,Chaoran Xu,Meng Yu,Jing Tang,Xiangxiang Chu,Chao Shen,Chenhao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对视频续接（video continuation）中"世界状态"难以准确建模的问题，提出了评测基准 Statebench 与方法 Stateagent。Statebench 将续接状态分为三类：过去可见状态、遮挡过程状态、复杂转移状态；Stateagent 显式维护实体状态表征，依据新提示更新状态，并将预测的动作后状态锚定为未来末帧，再渲染下一段视频。实验显示该方法将全场景状态分数（SCS-All）从 45.2 提升至 69.3，并改善了分钟级故事生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成器通过拼接短片段或自回归扩展来构建长视频，其依赖的记忆（近期帧、关键帧、记忆库、缓存特征）只是保存了历史可见证据，却未能可靠地将这些证据转化为"世界状态接口"——即明确上一动作后世界中何者成立、在下一提示下应如何变化。某些状态必须从遮挡或隐式变化中推断，而非直接从已观察帧复制。由此引出一个被忽视的问题：给定历史视频、其提示与新提示，模型能否生成同时反映历史视频与新提示所决定状态的后继视频？

Method: 作者提出：(1) Statebench 基准，覆盖三类状态——past-visible（过去可见状态）、occluded-process（遮挡过程状态）、complex-transition（复杂转移状态）；(2) Stateagent 方法，显式维护实体状态表示，在新提示下更新该状态，将预测的动作后状态锚定为未来的结束帧（end frame），并据此渲染下一段视频，从而实现受控的视频续接。

Result: 实验表明，Stateagent 将受控视频续接的全场景状态分数（SCS-All）从 45.2 提升至 69.3，同时也有助于一分钟量级的故事生成。

Conclusion: 通过显式的实体状态建模与未来末帧锚定，可以弥合历史视频记忆与下一提示所需世界状态之间的鸿沟，显著提升视频续接的状态一致性，并为长视频/故事生成提供新的有效手段。代码已开源。

Abstract: Video generators build long videos by composing shorter parts, either by generating segments one after another or by autoregressively extending chunks. Each new part usually depends on memories of historical observations, such as recent frames, selected key frames, memory banks, or cached features. These memories preserve visible evidence from the past, but current generators do not reliably turn such evidence into a world-state interface: what holds in the video world after previous actions and how it should change under the next prompt. A past frame remains valid history, but it may not describe the state needed by the next segment; some states must instead be inferred from occluded or implicit changes rather than copied from a directly observed frame. This creates a simple but overlooked question for video continuation: given a previous video, its prompt, and a new prompt, can a model generate a continuation that reflects the state determined by both the historical video and the new prompt? To answer this question, we introduce Statebench, a benchmark that targets this gap by testing continuations over three state categories: past-visible states, occluded-process states, and complex-transition states. We further propose Stateagent, which explicitly maintains an entity-state representation, updates it under the new prompt, grounds the predicted post-action state as a future end frame, and renders the next video. Experiments show that our method improves controlled video continuation by raising the all-case state score (SCS-All) from 45.2 to 69.3, and also benefits story generation at the one-minute scale. Code is avaliable at https://github.com/AMAP-ML/StateAgent.

</details>


### [53] [CoFiE: Coarse-to-Fine Evidence Selection for Efficient Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03675)
*Jing Jiang,Yiran Ling,Ruonan Li,Dimitrios Stamoulis,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoFiE 是一个面向流式视频理解的新型"由粗到细证据选择"框架，在视觉编码之前先进行与查询无关的粗粒度过滤（Novelty-Guided Frame Filtering），再在 LLM 预填充阶段进行与查询相关的细粒度精炼（Query-Specific Evidence Refinement），从而在低延迟约束下兼顾准确率与效率，并在多个视频理解基准上取得新的准确率-效率最优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频理解方法通过 token 剪枝和 memory-bank 方案提升效率，但这些方法主要在视觉编码之后减少视觉 token，无法从根本上降低端到端延迟，因为昂贵的帧编码开销已经发生。因此需要在视觉编码之前就过滤冗余帧，同时保留查询相关的细粒度选择能力。

Method: CoFiE 将证据选择解耦为两个阶段：1) 粗粒度、与查询无关的过滤阶段（视觉编码之前），采用 Novelty-Guided Frame Filtering 保留视觉上具有区分度的候选帧；2) 细粒度、与查询相关的精炼阶段（LLM 预填充期间），采用 Query-Specific Evidence Refinement 选择与用户查询最相关的帧。该设计在帧编码前去除大量冗余，同时利用语义信息进行查询相关的精炼。

Result: 在多个视频理解基准上，CoFiE 达到 StreamingBench 78.86% 和 OvO-Bench 68.72% 的准确率，相比先前方法提升最高达 3.15%；即使过滤高达 80% 的证据帧，仍优于强开源多模态模型，并将端到端推理延迟提升（降低）最多 2.54 倍。

Conclusion: CoFiE 通过在视觉编码之前进行粗粒度帧过滤与在 LLM 预填充阶段进行查询相关精炼，有效避免了冗余帧的昂贵编码开销，显著改善了流式视频理解的端到端延迟并保持甚至提升准确率，建立了新的准确率-效率权衡最优水平。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires Vision Language Models (VLLMs) to process growing video streams and answer user questions under tight latency constraints. Existing methods improve efficiency through token pruning and memory-bank schemes, but mainly reduce visual tokens after visual encoding. Consequently, downstream token pruning alone cannot substantially reduce end-to-end latency because the expensive frame encoding cost has already been incurred. We propose CoFiE, a Coarse-to-Fine Evidence Selection framework that decouples evidence selection into a coarse, query-agnostic filtering stage before the vision encoder and a fine, query-specific refinement stage during LLM prefill. CoFiE introduces Novelty-Guided Frame Filtering to retain visually distinctive candidate frames and Query-Specific Evidence Refinement to select the frames most relevant to the user query. This design removes substantial redundancy before frame encoding while preserving query-specific refinement once semantic information becomes available. Experiments show that CoFiE establishes a new state-of-the-art accuracy-efficiency trade-off across multiple video understanding benchmarks, reaching 78.86% accuracy on StreamingBench and 68.72% on OvO-Bench, with improvements of up to 3.15% over prior methods. Even with up to 80% evidence-frame filtering, CoFiE outperforms strong open-source multimodal models while improving end-to-end inference latency by up to 2.54 times.

</details>
